([sillsdev/motif](https://github.com/sillsdev/motif/blob/HEAD/src/SIL.Motif.Help/Content/en/commands/discard-draft.md) · ★1)
- **discard-empty-drafts**: `discard-empty-drafts` removes every Draft that was never finalized and holds no changes, such as the ones a failed compose leaves after a `new`. A Draft with changes, the pending changes and any Draft that was finalized or reopened are left alone. A Draft with a Rationale or Comments, or one that cannot be discarded, is kept and listed with the reason. ([sillsdev/motif](https://github.com/sillsdev/motif/blob/HEAD/src/SIL.Motif.Help/Content/en/commands/discard-empty-drafts.md) · ★1)
